Voice only — does not change engineering opinions or judgment. ([jellehuibregtse/grug](https://github.com/jellehuibregtse/grug/tree/HEAD/skills/grug-speak) · ★1)
- **grugops**: The grugops factory dispatcher — route any software-delivery request through the Orchestrator (bootstrap, plan, implement, gate, UAT, release). ([abitwise/grugops](https://github.com/abitwise/grugops/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/grugops) · 2 copies)
- **grugops-gate**: Run the grugops PR quality gate — install, lint, typecheck, unit, build, e2e, bounded self-fix, then report. Recommendation only; never merges. ([abitwise/grugops](https://github.com/abitwise/grugops/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/grugops-gate))
- **grugops-map**: Map or bootstrap a repo with the grugops factory — greenfield scaffold or brownfield survey, then seed the factory state. ([abitwise/grugops](https://github.com/abitwise/grugops/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/grugops-map))
- **grugops-plan**: Plan work with the grugops factory — turn an idea into epics, or turn an epic into ready tickets. ([abitwise/grugops](https://github.com/abitwise/grugops/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/grugops-plan))
- **grugops-release**: Cut a release with the grugops Release Manager. Human-confirmed deploy only — a named human must approve the production deploy. ([abitwise/grugops](https://github.com/abitwise/grugops/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/grugops-release))
- **grugops-ticket**: Drive a ready ticket to a pull request with the grugops factory — implement on a branch, run the gate, stop at the PR. ([abitwise/grugops](https://github.com/abitwise/grugops/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/grugops-ticket))
- **grugops-uat**: Build a UAT pack with the grugops factory — assemble the acceptance scenarios a human runs before sign-off. ([abitwise/grugops](https://github.com/abitwise/grugops/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/grugops-uat))
- **grumpy-pm-agent**: Mitchell Agentic Sprint Mini Council voice — the Grumpy PM. Adversarial-by-default operational-feasibility persona who's seen every "we'll just X" plan fail and assumes barriers multiply, not fall. Operates with maximum context isolation. Invoke during Mini Council orchestration at Step 1→2 and Step 4→5 transitions, or directly via /sprint-council. ([cgbarlow/mitchell-agentic-sprint](https://github.com/cgbarlow/mitchell-agentic-sprint/tree/HEAD/skills/grumpy-pm-agent) · ★1)
- **grumpy-review**: This skill should be used when the user requests a code review, audit, or quality assessment covering quality, security, dependencies, and documentation. It uses parallel agents and produces a deduplicated, severity-ranked report. ([lklimek/claudius](https://github.com/lklimek/claudius/tree/HEAD/skills/grumpy-review) · ★1)
- **grumpy-reviewer**: A single grumpy, nitpicky structural code reviewer that runs as an isolated subagent and treats the code as third-party work submitted by a junior programmer for validation. It cares about exactly one thing — maintainability — judged through separation of concerns, service-oriented design, helper-method extraction, small files, and the rule of 7 (as any grouping nears 7 members, it pushes for sub-groupings). It is deliberately kept OUT of the implementation flow: it never learns how the code was produced, only a one-line statement of what it is supposed to do, and its report is plain and fact… ([mhylle/claude-skills-collection](https://github.com/mhylle/claude-skills-collection/tree/HEAD/skills/grumpy-reviewer) · ★19)

Dafür liefert 'gs crosswalk &lt;ID&gt;' heuristische Top-Kandidaten (Token-Überlappung). Der alt-identifier-Diff funktioniert nur zwischen zwei Ständen DERSELBEN Edition. Nutzen bei Migration, Delta-Analyse oder Mapping auf andere Standards (z.B. ISO 27001).
